# R13: the relaxed history input remains open; two exact restrictions on its closure

**All new mathematics in this packet is UNVERIFIED under Danus policy. No all-depth history certificate, actual counterexample to that input, or original-target resolution is claimed.**

Grant `1a5403f680a965c1`: one native analytical invocation, two substantive approaches, mathematical programs/arrays/solvers zero, formal submissions zero, additional agents/models zero. The parent owns ledgers and shared administration. This packet contains manual derivations and text/hash operations only.

The conditional primitive--dominant joining calculation passes the algebraic checks below. The missing all-depth history inequality is not obtained. Two conclusions do narrow its continuation:

1. An actual asymmetric-Huber history rejects every proposed history composition that retains the *weakened* left residual with coefficient at least one and then adds only nonnegative residuals, original interpolation rows, and a PSD remainder. This excludes the full non-diagonal bridge/right-row family derived below, not just its diagonal specialization. The history input itself remains positive on this witness.
2. For every fixed finite internal depth, the requested weakened history inequality holds for all sufficiently large enclosing parameters. This is a dimension-uniform, nonnumerical consequence of strict coercivity at the limiting Silver word. Hence an actual failure along enclosing parameters tending to infinity must have internal depth tending to infinity. In particular, a fixed-depth negative-history trajectory that then stalls at a minimizer cannot furnish the requested obstruction. A separate exact argument excludes the proposed farther-minimizer stall mechanism for all cone-projection histories at every physical prefix.

## 1. Scope, parameters, and evidence

The original target is the exact comparison `lim W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1`, or a rigorous negative resolution on the original full function class. Keep `W = 2R`, all integer splits in the benchmark `B_n`, every finite dimension, every original selected minimizer and radius, and the actual last iterate. The accepted benchmark comparison reported in the supplied handoff does not supply the missing upper certificate.

Fix one enclosing integer `m >= 1536` and set

\[
\epsilon=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon),\quad a=\beta/c,
\quad H_0=(),\quad H_{j+1}=(H_j,1+ac^j,H_j).
\]

Every child retains these same parameters. Write `N_j = 2^j - 1`, `S_j = sum H_j`, and `C_j = 1 + S_j`. For a word of mass `s`, endpoint `v`, and endpoint gradient `p`, put

\[
E_\Gamma
=2\sum_i h_i(f_i-f_v)
-\sum_i h_i(h_i-1)\|g_i\|^2-\Gamma\|p\|^2.
\tag{1.1}
\]

The exact requested coefficient is

\[
\Gamma_j=\vartheta C_j^2-C_j,\qquad \vartheta=7/10.
\tag{1.2}
\]

Use the nonnegative original interpolation rows

\[
Q_{ij}=2f_i-2f_j-2\langle g_j,x_i-x_j\rangle-\|g_i-g_j\|^2.
\tag{1.3}
\]

These inequalities are necessary for every convex 1-smooth history. The arguments below do not invoke sufficiency of a numerical interpolation system. When an original minimizer is translated to zero, use

\[
Q_{\star i}=-2f_i+2\langle g_i,x_i\rangle-\|g_i\|^2.
\]

Telescoping physical squared distances gives the exact full-class identity

\[
E_\Gamma=\|x_{\rm start}\|^2-\sum_i h_iQ_{\star i}
-2s f_v-\|v\|^2-\Gamma\|p\|^2.
\tag{1.4}
\]

Input hashes checked exactly:

- TASK: `9ef075dfc458d2526b56399ec10c611bdf2932cd8c1a0e35efbf1f41b2bde08b`.
- Parent relaxed PD note: `f7a80cbc05ee2da4b168f61fbc307817550865fa94978fcbfe067f1dae125c09`.

Complete designated source reads include OPERATOR, PROBLEM, R12_FINAL_HANDOFF, R12_RELAXED_PD_JOIN_LEAD, the R11 two-input proof (in particular Sections 3--4), the old dense-history report and root derivation, and the R11 variable-interval note (in particular its conic-hull exclusion). The R7 and relevant R11 shared handoffs were also read to distinguish older failed assertions from the present input. The memory registry was used only for the evidence/authorization boundary; current files and the fresh grant control this invocation.

## 2. Check of the conditional PD join

Let the left physical mass be `s`, the bridge be `b = 1 + k`, its query be `v` with gradient `p`, and the right start be `w = v - bp`. Let the right dominance vector have total `B_R` and weighted gradient `U`. Define its true retained residual by

\[
D_R=\|w\|^2-\sum_R u_iQ_{\star i}-2B_R f_y-\|w-U\|^2.
\]

The original-row identity

\[
Q_{vi}=Q_{\star i}+2f_v-\|p\|^2+2\langle g_i,p-v\rangle
\]

gives, with `A_Q = sum_R u_i Q_vi`,

\[
2B_R(f_v-f_y)=B_R\|p\|^2+2k\langle p,U\rangle+\|U\|^2+D_R+A_Q.
\tag{2.1}
\]

For the parent vector `(h_left, gamma, tau*u_right)`, direct expansion of its dominance residual first gives

\[
\begin{aligned}
D_P={}&E_L+\tau D_R+2(s+\gamma)(f_v-f_y)
+(\Gamma+\gamma-\gamma^2)\|p\|^2\\
&+2\tau(b-\gamma)\langle p,U\rangle-\tau(\tau-1)\|U\|^2.
\end{aligned}
\]

Substituting (2.1) produces the stated parent formula, with

\[
A_q=\Gamma+s+2\gamma-\gamma^2,\quad
B_q=(s+\gamma)k/B_R+\tau(b-\gamma),\quad
C_q=(s+\gamma)/B_R-\tau(\tau-1).
\tag{2.2}
\]

For `C = 1+s`, `r = k/C`, `gamma = 1+3C/4`, `B_R = 1+(32/17)s`, and the prescribed parent total, set

\[
D_0=4(32-15/C),\qquad N=9+128r.
\]

The reductions in the parent note check:

\[
\tau=1+N/D_0,\quad A_q/C^2\ge11/80,
\quad C_q=(119-N)/D_0-N^2/D_0^2,
\]
\[
B_q/C=r-3/4+(128r^2+32r-27/4)/D_0.
\]

The actual ranges `C > 7/3`, `2/5 < r < 5/12` give the parent bounds `C_q > 9/50` and `|B_q| < C/7`, hence determinant greater than `851 C^2/196000`. These checks retain the actual child dominance residual and actual bridge/right rows. No higher primitive positivity was substituted.

The one-step identity also checks by expansion:

\[
E_{h(h+1)}=(h+1)Q_{01}+Q_{10}+(2-h^2)\|g_0-g_1\|^2.
\tag{2.3}
\]

The actual leaf has `1 < h < sqrt(2)`. Both requested leaf inputs follow with the exact differences stated in the parent note. This establishes only the conditional interface; it does not establish any non-leaf instance of (1.2).

## 3. Approach 1: a full non-diagonal original-row join and its actual obstruction

### 3.1 General retained identity

Consider equal child mass `s`, set

\[
C=1+s,\quad b=1+k,\quad T=C+k,\quad C_P=2C+k.
\]

The physical path is `x -> v -> w -> y`, with bridge `w = v-bp`, `p = g_v`, `q = g_y`, and right displacement

\[
G=w-y=\sum_R h_i g_i.
\]

Give both children the same coefficient `Gamma`, and give the parent `Gamma_P`. The elementary history split is

\[
E_P=E_L+E_R+2T(f_v-f_y)
+(\Gamma-bk)\|p\|^2+(\Gamma-\Gamma_P)\|q\|^2.
\tag{3.1}
\]

Keep the actual nonnegative sums

\[
A_v=\sum_R h_iQ_{vi},\qquad A_y=\sum_R h_iQ_{yi}.
\]

Squared-distance telescoping on the right child, with no sign assumption on its residual, gives

\[
\begin{aligned}
A_v={}&2s(f_v-f_y)-E_R-\Gamma\|q\|^2
-\|G\|^2-2k\langle p,G\rangle-s\|p\|^2,\\
A_y={}&-E_R+\|G\|^2+2\langle q,G\rangle-(s+\Gamma)\|q\|^2.
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.2}
\]

The endpoint rows are

\[
Q_{vy}=2(f_v-f_y)-\|p\|^2-2k\langle p,q\rangle-2\langle q,G\rangle-\|q\|^2,
\]
\[
Q_{yv}=-2(f_v-f_y)+(1+2k)\|p\|^2+2\langle p,G\rangle+2\langle p,q\rangle-\|q\|^2.
\tag{3.3}
\]

Choose nonnegative `lambda, mu, t, u` satisfying

\[
t-u=T-\lambda s,\qquad B=1+\lambda+\mu.
\]

Then the exact original-row representation is

\[
E_P=E_L+B E_R+\lambda A_v+\mu A_y+tQ_{vy}+uQ_{yv}
+\mathcal R(p,q,G),
\tag{3.4}
\]

where the symmetric coefficient matrix of the full quadratic remainder has entries

\[
\begin{aligned}
R_{pp}&=\Gamma+C-k^2-2ku,\\
R_{qq}&=\Gamma-\Gamma_P+\lambda\Gamma+\mu(s+\Gamma)+t+u,\\
R_{GG}&=\lambda-\mu,\\
R_{pq}&=tk-u,\quad R_{pG}=\lambda k-u,\quad R_{qG}=t-\mu.
\end{aligned}
\tag{3.5}
\]

The off-diagonal entries multiply twice the corresponding inner products. Thus this attempt genuinely retains a non-diagonal remainder and the right child's signed history. It is not the excluded positive interval/diagonal-slack construction.

Setting

\[
\lambda=\mu=\ell=\frac{C+k}{C-k},\qquad t=\ell,\quad u=k\ell,
\quad B=1+2\ell
\]

eliminates the displacement variable and mixed coefficients. If `J = Gamma+C` and `J_P = Gamma_P+C_P`, the resulting exact identity is

\[
E_P=E_L+B E_R+\ell(A_v+A_y+Q_{vy}+kQ_{yv})
+(J-Bk^2)\|p\|^2+(BJ-J_P)\|q\|^2.
\tag{3.6}
\]

For the standard coefficients `J=C^2`, `J_P=C_P^2`, this becomes

\[
E_P^{\rm std}=E_L^{\rm std}+B E_R^{\rm std}
+\ell(A_v+A_y+Q_{vy}+kQ_{yv})
+\Delta(\|q\|^2-\|p\|^2),
\tag{3.7}
\]
\[
\Delta=Bk^2-C^2
=C^2\frac{(1+r)(r^2+2r-1)}{1-r},\qquad r=k/C.
\]

This matches the signed-defect identity sent independently by the parent. In particular, its positive defect at super-Silver ratios cannot be discarded, and its right history residual cannot be assumed nonnegative at the perturbed standard coefficient.

### 3.2 A literal same-enclosing-parameter trajectory

For any actual child depth `j >= 3`, write `C = C_j`, `k = a c^j`, and `r = k/C`. The exact capacity formula is

\[
C_j=\frac{a}{c-2}c^j+\left(1-\frac{a}{c-2}\right)2^j.
\]

Consequently

\[
r=\frac{c-2}{1+\zeta(2/c)^j},\quad
\zeta=\frac{(5+2\sqrt2)\epsilon+(1+\sqrt2)^2\epsilon^2}{1-\epsilon},
\quad 0<\zeta<9\epsilon.
\]

With `c_0 = 1+sqrt(2)` and `r_0 = 1/c_0`, for `j >= 3`,

\[
r_0\zeta(2/c)^j<72\epsilon/c_0^4<c_0\epsilon,
\]

because `c_0^5 = 41+29sqrt(2) > 72`. As `c-2 = r_0+c_0 epsilon`, this proves

\[
r>r_0,\qquad r(2+r)>1.
\tag{3.8}
\]

Use the single static scalar function

\[
f_r(z)=
\begin{cases}
-rz-r^2/2,&z\le-r,\\
z^2/2,&-r\le z\le1,\\
z-1/2,&z\ge1.
\end{cases}
\tag{3.9}
\]

It is convex, differentiable, 1-smooth, and has unique minimizer zero. Its gradient is `clamp(z,-r,1)`. Take the original start `x_0=C`, so the original radius is exactly `C`.

For a left physical prefix of mass `T_i`, its query is `C-T_i >= 1`, with gradient one. The left child ends at `v=p=1`. The physical bridge sends this to `w=-k`. For the corresponding right prefix, its query is

\[
-k+rT_i\le-r,
\]

with gradient `-r`, and the right child ends exactly at `y=q=-r`, because `k=Cr`. These formulas cover every physical query of the literal word `H_(j+1)`, with no child reset or fictitious sample. Values are given by (3.9). Every minimizer row `Q_star,i` vanishes.

For the requested weakened coefficient on each child, put `delta=1-vartheta=3/10`. The exact residuals are

\[
E_L=\delta C^2,\qquad E_R=\delta C^2r^2,
\]
\[
E_P=C^2\{1-\vartheta[r(2+r)]^2\}.
\tag{3.10}
\]

All rows `Q_vi`, `Q_yi` for right queries, and both endpoint rows `Q_vy,Q_yv`, vanish on this history. More importantly,

\[
E_P-E_L=\vartheta C^2\{1-[r(2+r)]^2\}<0.
\tag{3.11}
\]

Thus no identity or universal inequality of the following form can hold with all displayed terms nonnegative:

\[
E_P\ \ge\ \alpha E_L+\beta_R E_R
+\sum_{i,l}\lambda_{il}Q_{il}+\mathcal P,
\quad \alpha\ge1,\ \beta_R\ge0,\ \lambda_{il}\ge0,\ \mathcal P\ge0.
\tag{3.12}
\]

Here the interpolation rows may be *any* original rows, and `P` may be any PSD quadratic remainder on all original gradient variables. The contradiction is the actual inequality `E_P < E_L`; it does not depend on freeing matrix variables or on a negative eigenvalue of a chosen ansatz. In particular, every choice in (3.4)--(3.5) fails as a nonnegative certificate for this same weakened-coefficient induction.

This exclusion is available at every enclosing `m >= 1536`: take `j=3` to test `H_4`, or `j=m-2` to obtain an unbounded inherited family inside the required range `j+1<m`. It does not exclude a direct whole-word certificate, a genuinely different simultaneous invariant, a coefficient below one on the weak left residual, a stronger left input, or an identity retaining signed quantitative surplus.

### 3.3 The witness does not refute the requested input

The actual ratio satisfies `r < 5/12`. Therefore

\[
\frac{E_P}{C^2}
>1-\frac7{10}\left(\frac{145}{144}\right)^2
=\frac{60185}{207360}>\frac{29}{100}.
\tag{3.13}
\]

Both children and the parent have strictly positive weakened residuals. The obstruction is to paying for this weak parent with the weak child quantities in (3.12). At the limiting Silver ratio, `E_P=E_L` and `E_R=delta C^2 r_0^2>0`; even there, retaining the left residual in full leaves no room for a positive multiple of the weak right residual on this equality history. At actual inherited ratios, (3.11) is strict.

The fixed three-tenths terminal margin is not defeated by the known saturated-root mechanism. This calculation records that fact quantitatively rather than calling the trajectory an input counterexample.

## 4. Approach 2: actual counterexample/stall analysis

The parent's suggested decisive negative construction was an actual initial `H_j` that reaches another minimizer, has zero terminal gradient, and leaves a negative history residual. Later steps would then stall, extending the same negative residual to arbitrarily deep complete words. The following results delimit that mechanism. No such counterexample is constructed.

### 4.1 Strict fixed-depth coercivity at the limiting Silver word

For this subsection only, let `epsilon=0`, so

\[
c_0=1+\sqrt2,\quad a_0=1/c_0,\quad
C_j^0=c_0^j,\quad k_j^0=a_0 C_j^0.
\]

This is a limiting word used in a continuity argument, not a replacement for the actual inherited parameters.

At these parameters `Delta=0` in (3.7). The leaf identity (2.3), with `h=sqrt(2)`, and induction through (3.7) give

\[
E_j^{\rm std}\ge0,\qquad
\Gamma_j^{\rm std}=(C_j^0)^2-C_j^0,
\tag{4.1}
\]

for every physical interpolation-feasible dataset. The proof uses only nonnegative original rows. It proves (4.1) at the exact limiting word and makes no assertion of higher standard primitive positivity at positive epsilon.

We need the equality statement, not merely (4.1):

> If `E_j^std = 0` and the terminal gradient is zero, then every gradient in that word is zero.

For the leaf, equality in (2.3) gives `Q_01=Q_10=0`. With `g_1=0`, their sum is

\[
Q_{01}+Q_{10}=2(h-1)\|g_0\|^2,
\]

so `g_0=0`. For a parent, equality in (3.7) gives zero child residuals and zero endpoint rows, since all their coefficients are positive. The right child, whose terminal gradient is zero, has every gradient zero by induction. Thus `G=0` and `w=y`. The bridge/terminal row sum then is

\[
Q_{vy}+Q_{yv}=2k\|p\|^2.
\]

It must vanish, hence `p=0`. The left induction finishes the proof. This argument uses just the original interpolation inequalities and physical displacement identities, so it also applies to a feasible finite Gram representation without first constructing a realizing function.

The weakened residual at the limiting word satisfies

\[
E_j^{\rm weak}=E_j^{\rm std}+\frac3{10}(C_j^0)^2\|g_{N_j}\|^2.
\tag{4.2}
\]

Consequently it can vanish on interpolation-feasible data only when *all* gradients vanish.

Here is a precise dimension-uniform coercivity consequence. Fix `j`, put `n=N_j`, normalize

\[
\sum_{i=0}^n\|g_i\|^2=1,
\]

and represent the gradients by their `(n+1)`-square Gram matrix `M`, with `M PSD` and `trace M=1`. Translate the terminal position and value to zero in the interpolation system. Physical positions are then determined by

\[
x_i-x_n=\sum_{t=i}^{n-1}h_t g_t.
\]

The two endpoint rows imply

\[
\langle g_n,x_i-x_n\rangle+\tfrac12\|g_i-g_n\|^2
\le f_i-f_n
\le\langle g_i,x_i-x_n\rangle-\tfrac12\|g_i-g_n\|^2.
\tag{4.3}
\]

Since each gradient norm is at most one, these function differences have absolute value at most the fixed word mass `S_j^0`. Hence the set of such normalized feasible Gram/value data is compact: PSD trace-one matrices form a compact set, the values are bounded, and all interpolation constraints are closed. It is nonempty, for example from a nonconstant quadratic history followed by normalization.

The continuous functional (4.2) has strictly positive value everywhere on this compact set, by the equality argument and `trace M=1`. It therefore has a positive minimum `eta_j`. Homogeneity gives

\[
E_j^{\rm weak}\ge\eta_j\sum_{i=0}^{N_j}\|g_i\|^2
\qquad(\epsilon=0),\qquad \eta_j>0.
\tag{4.4}
\]

No numerical optimization, solver, or finite-array calculation is hidden in this compactness proof. The finite Gram size removes dependence on the original ambient dimension.

### 4.2 Transfer to actual enclosing parameters; no fixed-depth obstruction

For each fixed `j`, the actual coefficients `H_j(m)`, `S_j(m)`, and `Gamma_j(m)` converge to their limiting values as `m -> infinity`. Suppose there were a sequence of enclosing parameters tending to infinity and actual histories at this fixed depth with

\[
E_j^{\rm weak}<\tfrac12\eta_j\sum_i\|g_i\|^2.
\]

A nonzero violating history can be normalized to gradient trace one. The physical masses remain uniformly bounded because `j` is fixed; (4.3) therefore gives a uniform value bound. Extract a convergent subsequence of the finite Gram matrices and values. The interpolation constraints and the history functional are continuous in these data and in the physical step coefficients. The limit is an interpolation-feasible normalized dataset for the exact Silver word with residual at most `eta_j/2`, contradicting (4.4).

Thus the following statement is established in this manual draft:

\[
\boxed{\text{For every fixed }j\ge1\text{, there is }M_j<\infty
\text{ such that for all }m\ge M_j,
\quad E_j\ge\tfrac12\eta_j\sum_i\|g_i\|^2.}
\tag{4.5}
\]

It holds for all finite dimensions and all original convex 1-smooth histories, with the actual same-enclosing-m coefficients. A finite collection of depths can be handled by taking the maximum of the corresponding thresholds. Therefore, if the requested all-depth input fails along a sequence `m -> infinity`, its violating depth must tend to infinity along a subsequence; bounded-depth failures are impossible eventually.

This also excludes the parent's fixed-depth absorbing-prefix mechanism: on a history ending at a minimizer the terminal gradient penalty is zero, and a negative history residual contradicts (4.5). For a projection body whose minimizer rows vanish, the special proposed farther-minimizer condition would be exactly such a negative residual.

The order of quantifiers matters. We have `for every fixed j, eventually in m`, not `eventually in m, for every j<m`. No bound on the growth of `M_j` or decay of `eta_j` is supplied. Equation (4.5) is a proved restriction on possible counterexamples, not the task's all-depth input or a substitute for it.

### 4.3 A separate all-prefix exclusion for cone projections

There is also an exact exclusion of farther-minimizer absorption for the whole cone-projection subclass, at arbitrary inherited depth. Let `K` be a closed convex cone and

\[
f(x)=\tfrac12\|P_Kx\|^2,
\qquad \arg\min f=K^\circ.
\]

Write `g=P_Kx`, `n=x-g`. The projection optimality conditions for a cone give `n in K^polar`, `<n,g>=0`, and, for any vector `z`, `<z,P_Kz>=||P_Kz||^2`. These follow by testing the projection condition against zero and positive multiples of the projected vector.

For `h>1` and `r=h-1`, the next gradient `g^+=P_K(n-rg)` obeys

\[
\|g^+\|^2=\langle g^+,n-rg\rangle
\le-r\langle g^+,g\rangle\le r\|g^+\|\|g\|.
\]

Hence `||g^+|| <= r||g||`. For any originally selected minimizer `z_star in K^polar`,

\[
\|x-z_\star\|^2-\|x-hg-z_\star\|^2
\ge h(2-h)\|g\|^2.
\tag{4.6}
\]

Define physical prefix products `P_0=1`, `P_t=prod_(i<t)(h_i-1)`. Every prefix of every actual inherited `H_j` satisfies `P_t <= 1`. To prove this, the total product is

\[
P(H_j)=\beta^{2^j-1}c^{-j},
\tag{4.7}
\]

by induction on the concatenation. Prefixes in the left child obey the claim inductively. After that child and its bridge, the product is

\[
P(H_j)\,a c^j=a\beta^{2^j-1}<1,
\]

and multiplication by any right-child prefix again preserves the bound.

Set `v_i=||g_i||^2/P_i^2`. These numbers are nonnegative and nonincreasing. For every physical prefix length `T >= 1`, summation by parts gives

\[
\begin{aligned}
\sum_{i<T}h_i(2-h_i)\|g_i\|^2
={}&(1-P_T^2)v_{T-1}\\
&+\sum_{i=0}^{T-2}(1-P_{i+1}^2)(v_i-v_{i+1})\ge0.
\end{aligned}
\tag{4.8}
\]

Combining (4.6)--(4.8) yields

\[
\boxed{\|x_T-z_\star\|\le\|x_0-z_\star\|
\quad\text{for every physical prefix and every original }z_\star\in K^\circ.}
\tag{4.9}
\]

This is a distance bound from the original start at each prefix; it does not claim the distance decreases at each individual long step. It rules out reaching a more distant selected-minimizer point in this entire subclass, even if the terminal objective is zero. The same conclusion holds for a compact cone truncation whenever its cap is nonbinding on the complete history; its polar minimizer set is unchanged.

The prior R11 cone result concerned objective decay and the closest polar-minimizer radius. Equation (4.9) addresses every originally selected polar minimizer and the proposed stall defect. It does not exclude a bounded origin-vertex body whose cap becomes active, a nonhomogeneous body, or other smooth-convex functions. No general-history inequality follows from it.

# R13 fixed increasing weights: two exact scope results, no full certificate

**UNVERIFIED analytical draft. Grant 9cb17568ad79648c. Original target OPEN.**

This packet does not construct an admissible general interpolation matrix with a PSD full remainder, and it does not produce an actual trajectory with negative prescribed dominance residual. The two authorized approaches give the following narrower results.

1. The whole positive cone of variable-step interval modes is excluded for the FIXED increasing physical weights, even when the Laplacian and signed diagonal terms are retained together. This is a new derivation with the actual columns `b_i=u_i`; the old result with `b_i=h_i` is not imported. The contradiction uses a necessary diagonal condition of the FULL remainder, not separately nonnegative diagonal slack after removing the Laplacian.
2. A substantial family of actual symmetric-Huber complete trajectories, allowing arbitrary admissible inner phases at depth `floor(m/2)`, has a uniformly positive residual of order `S^2`. Thus changing the inner phase of that saturated outer recursion cannot provide the requested actual obstruction. This is a calibration on a named family, not a full-class validity result.

The positive interval cone does not exhaust arbitrary admissible cyclic matrices. The first result therefore leaves genuinely nonseparable circulations open. The original minimax question and the fixed increasing dominance functional remain unresolved.

## 1. Sources, authorization, and locked notation

Read completely: OPERATOR.md; PROBLEM.md; R12_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md; R12_EXCESS_WEIGHT_LAPLACIAN_LEAD_20261009.md; R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md; R12_AB_COUNTEREXAMPLE_HANDOFF_20261009.md; brownian_dominance_certificate_20261009_r12/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md; profile_c_full_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md; and R11_VARIABLE_INTERVAL_CERTIFICATE_LEAD_20261009.md, including its complete Section 5. The mathematical-academic-writing skill and its mathematical-integrity reference were used for evidence and quantifier discipline. The initialized marker is present. No private worker memory was read.

Checked input hashes:

- TASK.md: `abd19f8e86df1dcabcc1afe63cc014ea96ed2277831ee71ed9b4310a743de6e6`.
- R12_EXCESS_WEIGHT_LAPLACIAN_LEAD_20261009.md: `af20644806bbe030993835b5b4258e005a0fc0b30f64835ea68a6c51dd0e6593`.
- R12_PARENT_RANKONE_FLOW_OBSTRUCTION_20261009.md: `870d3f51efa4f7ddb01d6e2632885f578c920e5da7940ba32200d5de77e88eb8`.

The exact original question remains

    lim_(n->infinity) W_n^*/U_n^ZJ = 1,

or a rigorous disproof by a full-class asymptotic separation. Here `W=2R`, and `U_n^ZJ=1/B_n`, where `B_n=2 S_bullet(n)+1` is the EXACT sequence with maximization over all integer splits from PROBLEM.md. The class includes every finite-dimensional convex differentiable L-smooth function with an attained minimum, every original selected minimizer and distinct initial point, and the actual last iterate of every predetermined nonnegative schedule. Nothing below changes that class, radius, benchmark, or endpoint.

Fix ONE enclosing integer `m>=1536` and retain throughout every child

    c0=1+sqrt(2), epsilon=1/(4m), beta=1-epsilon,
    c=c0(1+epsilon), a=beta/c,
    H_0=empty, H_(j+1)=(H_j,1+a c^j,H_j).

Write

    k_j=a c^j, s_j=sum H_j, n=2^m-1, h=H_m,
    S=s_m, T_i=sum_(t<i) h_t,
    Bcert=1+(32/17)S,
    u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i for i<n,
    u_n=Bcert-sum_(i<n)u_i.

`Bcert` is not `B_n`. No physical step is added at query n. Put

    V=sum_(i<n) h_i^2,
    U=sum_(i<n)u_i=5S/4-V/(4S),
    u_n=1+(43/68)S+V/(4S)>0,
    b_i=u_i for i<n, b_n=-U.

Let Bmat have diagonal 1 and entries `(Bmat)_ij=h_j` for `j<i`. For normalized L=1 trajectories use the NONNEGATIVE rows

    Q_ij=2f_i-2f_j-2<g_j,x_i-x_j>-||g_i-g_j||^2,
    Q_star,i=-2(f_i-f_star)+2<g_i,x_i-x_star>-||g_i||^2.

The unchanged functional and full remainder are

    D_u=||x_0-x_star||^2-sum_i u_i Q_star,i
        -2Bcert(f_n-f_star)-||x_0-x_star-sum_i u_i g_i||^2,

    R=L+diag(u_i(1-u_i)+b_i-L_ii)
      -(C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T),

    D_u=sum_(i,j) C_ji Q_ij+g^T R g.                 (1.1)

Here C has zero row sums, nonnegative off-diagonal entries, and column sums b. The Laplacian has weights `w_ij=u_j(u_i-h_i)` for `i<j`. Its diagonal cancels from the full diagonal in (1.1); in particular, if `H=C Bmat+Bmat^T C^T`, then

    R_ii=u_i(2-u_i)-H_ii,       i<n.                (1.2)

Equation (1.2) is a necessary PSD test regardless of signs in any decomposition of R.

## 2. Approach 1: exclude the positive interval-mode cone at the actual weights

### 2.1 Explicit index-dependent modes and the precise family

For each atom nu, let `A_i^nu` be nonnegative and nondecreasing, with `A_-1^nu=0`, and let `D_i^nu` be nonnegative and nonincreasing, with `D_(n+1)^nu=0`. Interval-supported atoms are included by extending their cumulative weights by zero or constants outside their support. Absorb a nonnegative combination coefficient into A. Set `r_i=h_i-1` at physical indices. For `i<j`, the actual entries of an atom are

    C_ji^nu=(A_i^nu-A_(i-1)^nu)(D_j^nu+r_j D_(j+1)^nu),
    C_ij^nu=(A_(i-1)^nu+r_i A_i^nu)(D_j^nu-D_(j+1)^nu).

At j=n the dummy r_n multiplies zero. Diagonal entries make row sums zero. All off-diagonal entries are nonnegative. The sum `C=sum_nu C^nu` is an actual, generally index-dependent cyclic coefficient family, although no feasible member with the target columns and PSD remainder is asserted.

Use any finite number of atoms, with arbitrary m-dependent coefficients and supports. This includes positive sums with different interval destinations and does not assume one rank-one destination law.

For this family the symmetric product H is diagonal. Define

    Z_i=sum_nu A_i^nu D_(i+1)^nu,
    q_i=1+2 sum_nu A_(i-1)^nu D_i^nu,
    B_i^nu=sum_(t<i) h_t A_t^nu,
    E_i^nu=sum_(t>=i,t<n) h_t D_(t+1)^nu.

Thus `q_(i+1)=1+2Z_i`, and direct multiplication with the ACTUAL Bmat gives

    H_ii=2[r_i^2 Z_i-sum_nu A_(i-1)^nu D_i^nu].    (2.1)

The off-diagonal cancellation is the interval-mode identity in the source; it follows by writing the upper entry of `C Bmat` as the product of the corresponding forward cumulative factor and backward cumulative factor, and the lower entry as its negative.

### 2.2 The new column equations and full diagonal recurrence

Let `U_i=sum_(t<=i)u_t`, for physical i. The required column sums give

    sum_nu[A_i^nu E_i^nu-D_(i+1)^nu B_i^nu
                       -2r_i A_i^nu D_(i+1)^nu]=U_i. (2.2)

Here is a flow check of (2.2). Across the cut after i, an atom's forward outgoing mass is

    D_(i+1)^nu [B_i^nu+r_i A_i^nu],

and its backward incoming mass is

    A_i^nu [D_(i+1)^nu+E_(i+1)^nu].

Subtract the first from the second and use
`E_i^nu=h_i D_(i+1)^nu+E_(i+1)^nu`. Summing atoms gives the net incoming mass U_i. No individual atom is required to have the target columns.

Put

    delta_i=u_i-h_i=h_i T_i/(2S),
    e_i=U_i-(T_i+h_i)=sum_(t<=i)delta_t.

Combining (1.2) and (2.1) yields the FULL physical diagonal

    R_ii=q_i-r_i^2 q_(i+1)-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i).   (2.3)

The last term is essential. Increasing u changes the physical diagonal as well as the cut equations.

### 2.3 Generalized prefix identity, including the entire correction

For a physical cut k define

    Xi_k=sum_(i<k)[2h_i e_i-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)].

Then the exact identity is

    q_k+sum_(i<k)R_ii
      =(T_k+1)^2-2sum_nu B_k^nu E_k^nu+Xi_k.       (2.4)

To verify it, use

    B_(i+1)^nu=B_i^nu+h_i A_i^nu,
    E_(i+1)^nu=E_i^nu-h_i D_(i+1)^nu.

Equation (2.2) gives

    sum_nu(B_(i+1)^nu E_(i+1)^nu-B_i^nu E_i^nu)
      =h_i U_i+h_i(h_i-2)Z_i.

Therefore the increment of the right side of (2.4) before adding Xi is

    (1-r_i^2)q_(i+1)-2h_i e_i.

Adding the increment of Xi gives `(1-r_i^2)q_(i+1)-delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)`, which equals the increment of the left side by (2.3). Both sides initially equal 1. This proves (2.4) using only the combined column equations.

For a prefix with total physical mass `s=T_k`, set

    V_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^2,
    W_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^3,
    K_<k=sum_(i<k)h_i^2 T_i^2.

The FIXED increasing clock makes the correction explicit:

    Xi_k=[(s+1)(s^2-V_<k)/2-(s^3-W_<k)/3]/S
         -K_<k/(4S^2).                             (2.5)

For completeness, interchanging finite sums gives

    2sum_(i<k)h_i e_i
      =(1/S)sum_(i<k)h_i T_i(s-T_i).

Subtracting `sum delta_i(2r_i+delta_i)` leaves

    (1/S)sum_(i<k)h_i T_i(s+1-T_i-h_i)
      -K_<k/(4S^2).

Now use `sum h_i T_i=(s^2-V_<k)/2` and
`sum h_i T_i(T_i+h_i)=(s^3-W_<k)/3`. These prove (2.5) exactly, without a continuous-clock approximation.

Since `W_<k<=s V_<k` and `K_<k>=0`, (2.5) implies

    Xi_k<=s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).                        (2.6)

If the FULL R is PSD, each R_ii is nonnegative. Also `B_k^nu E_k^nu>=0`. Consequently (2.4) gives

    q_k<=(T_k+1)^2+Xi_k.                            (2.7)

No separate nonnegativity of `R-L`, of the signed diagonal in (1.1), or of an invented slack variable has been assumed.

### 2.4 Central cut contradiction

At the central query `k=2^(m-1)-1`, put

    s=s_(m-1), K=k_(m-1), h_k=1+K, S=2s+1+K.

The right physical suffix has total mass s. In each atom, monotonicity of D gives

    E_(k+1)^nu<=s D_(k+1)^nu.

Separating the bridge in (2.2) therefore gives

    U_k<= (s+1-K) Z_k.                              (2.8)

The omitted term `-sum D_(k+1)^nu B_k^nu` is nonpositive. Since `U_k>=s+1+K>0`, existence would force `s+1-K>0`, and

    q_(k+1)>=1+2(s+1+K)/(s+1-K).                   (2.9)

This lower bound deliberately uses only `u_i>=h_i`; retaining the full larger U_k would strengthen it.

At this bridge `delta_k=s(1+K)/(2S)`. Equations (2.3), (2.6), (2.7), and (2.9) imply the following necessary condition for the FULL R to be PSD:

    K^2[1+2(s+1+K)/(s+1-K)]+2K delta_k
      <=(s+1)^2+s^3/(6S)+s^2/(2S).                 (2.10)

The nonnegative `delta_k^2` has only been dropped from the left side.

Let `r=sqrt(2)-1`. The exact inherited-word sum is

    alpha=a/(c-2),
    s_j+1=alpha c^j+(1-alpha)2^j.                   (2.11)

Here alpha tends to 1 and is uniformly positive; `2/c<=2/c0<1`. Along the actual enclosing m, (2.11) gives

    K/s -> r, S/s -> c0=1/r,
    delta_k/s -> r^2/2, s -> infinity.

Divide (2.10) by s^2. Its limiting necessary condition is

    r^2[1+2(1+r)/(1-r)]+r^3 <= 1+r/6.

The identity `r^2+2r=1` makes the bracketed first contribution exactly 1. Thus it would require

    r^3<=r/6.

This is false: `6r^2>1` is equivalent to `17>12sqrt(2)`, and both sides are positive with squares `289>288`. The limiting contradiction has a strict constant gap. All quantities in (2.10) depend only on the literal word and prescribed weights, not on the atoms. Hence every finite positive interval-mode combination fails the full PSD requirement for all sufficiently large enclosing m, even if the number, supports, and weights of the atoms vary arbitrarily with m.

This proves a family obstruction. It does not prove that every admissible C has diagonal H, or that every nonnegative C with diagonal H belongs to this positive cone. Neither assertion is used. General C, and diagonal-H matrices outside this cone, remain unexcluded.

## 3. Approach 2: actual Huber inner-phase variation cannot reject the profile

### 3.1 A complete interval-image bound for the physical word

Use the SAME static function at every query,

    f(x)=x^2/2 if |x|<=1, and |x|-1/2 otherwise,
    g(x)=clip(x,[-1,1]), x_star=0.

It is convex, differentiable, 1-smooth, with unique original minimizer 0, and `Q_star,i=0` at every real query. Every conclusion in this section concerns this function only.

For `h>1`, the image of `[-R,R]` under `x -> x-h clip(x,[-1,1])` is the interval of radius

    Phi_h(R)=(h-1)R,                     0<=R<=1,
    Phi_h(R)=max(R-h,h-1),               R>=1.       (3.1)

This follows from continuity, oddness, and the three affine pieces of the update. In particular it is a statement about actual images, not a freely selected gradient table. For all R>=0,

    Phi_h(R)<=max(R-h,h-1).                         (3.2)

Define `t=a beta`. For every inherited depth j>=1,

    T_(H_j)([-k_j,k_j]) subset [-t,t].              (3.3)

Proof: at j=1, `k_1=beta<1`, so the one step `1+a` gives radius `a beta`. For j>=2, iterate (3.2) through the left H_(j-1). Its output radius is at most

    max(k_j-s_(j-1), k_(j-2)).

To see this, expansion of the scalar max recurrence subtracts the suffix mass from each reset value; each reset value is at most the largest `h-1` in that child, namely k_(j-2).

Both terms are at most `1+2k_(j-1)`. The only nontrivial inequality is

    k_j-s_(j-1)<=1+2k_(j-1),

equivalently `(c-2)k_(j-1)<=s_(j-1)+1`. Formula (2.11) proves it because `0<c-2<1`, so

    s_(j-1)+1>=k_(j-1)/(c-2)>=(c-2)k_(j-1).

Here `c<3` holds throughout the stated parameter range. The bridge of length `1+k_(j-1)` maps every interval of radius at most `1+2k_(j-1)` into `[-k_(j-1),k_(j-1)]`, by (3.1). The induction hypothesis on the final child then proves (3.3). In particular t<1, so every endpoint in (3.3) lies in the actual quadratic region.

### 3.2 Many admissible inner phases and exact outer execution

Set `J=floor(m/2)`. Define

    d_j=k_(j+1)-(s_j+1)
       =(beta-alpha)c^j-(1-alpha)2^j.

For the actual parameters, `0<alpha<1`,

    d_1=epsilon[2/(c0(1+epsilon))-c0 epsilon]>0,
    d_(j+1)=c d_j+(c-2)(1-alpha)2^j>d_j.            (3.4)

Choose ANY base start

    Y_J in [max(0,k_J-d_J), k_J].                   (3.5)

Execute the full physical H_J from that Y_J; no inner itinerary is prescribed. For j>=J define the outer starts by

    Y_(j+1)=s_j+1+k_j-Y_j.                          (3.6)

Let `e_j=k_j-Y_j`. At the base, `0<=e_J<=d_J`, and (3.6) gives

    Y_(j+1)=s_j+1+e_j,
    e_(j+1)=d_j-e_j.

Using (3.4), induction gives `0<=e_j<=d_j` at every later level. Consequently, at level j+1 the entire left H_j and bridge have actual gradient +1; the bridge query is `1+e_j`, and the bridge lands exactly at `-Y_j`. The remaining child is the negative of the complete H_j trajectory by oddness. This constructs every one of the complete H_m trajectories in (3.5)-(3.6), retaining every physical update and the original minimizer.

By (3.3), regardless of its inner phase, the endpoint satisfies

    |x_n|=|g_n|<=t.                                (3.7)

In particular the endpoint is not replaced by an arbitrary gradient of magnitude 1. Such a replacement would be infeasible for this family.

### 3.3 Uniform moments at the actual enclosing depth

Let the reference critical starts be

    X_1=beta,
    X_(j+1)=s_j+1+k_j-X_j.

The source's exact trajectory proof gives `0<X_j<=k_j`; it also follows from (3.4) with `k_1-X_1=0`. Put

    r=sqrt(2)-1,
    v=r^2/(1+2sqrt(2)).

The needed reference limits are

    X_m/S -> r,
    V_m/S^2 -> v,
    M_m^X/S^2 -> v,                                (3.8)

where `V_j=sum_(H_j) h_i^2` and `M_j^X=sum_(H_j)h_i x_(i+1)` along the reference trajectory. These are limits with the SAME enclosing parameters in all children.

For a direct check of their uniformity, write

    d(c)=(c-1)/(c+1),
    P=alpha c d(c), Q=2(1-alpha)/3, R0=beta-P-Q.

The exact finite-depth formulas are

    X_j=P c^(j-1)+Q 2^(j-1)+R0(-1)^(j-1),
    V_j=(2^j-1)+2alpha(c^j-2^j)
        +a^2(c^(2j)-2^j)/(c^2-2).                  (3.9)

The moment satisfies

    M_(j+1)^X+M_j^X
      =s_j X_(j+1)-(s_j^2+V_j)/2-(1+k_j)X_j.      (3.10)

The left-child term is the sum of `h_i(X_(j+1)-T_i-h_i)`, the bridge term is `-(1+k_j)X_j`, and the right child contributes `-M_j^X`. With

    v(c)=(c-2)^2/(c^2-2),
    w(c)=[2d(c)-(1+v(c))/2]/(c^2+1),

(2.11), (3.9), and (3.10) give

    X_j=d(c)alpha c^j+O(2^j),
    V_j=v(c)alpha^2 c^(2j)+O(c^j+2^j),
    M_j^X=w(c)alpha^2 c^(2j)+O((2c)^j).

All constants are uniform for c in the compact interval
`[c0,c0(1+1/6144)]`. For the last estimate, subtract its stated leading term from (3.10); the resulting error plus its predecessor is `O((2c)^j)`, whose finite geometric sum has the same order uniformly. Finally `d(c0)=r`, `v(c0)=v`, and `w(c0)=v`. This proves (3.8) without exchanging a fixed-depth and enclosing-depth limit.

Now compare the starts (3.5)-(3.6) with X. For j>=J,

    Y_j-X_j=(-1)^(j-J)(Y_J-X_J),
    |Y_J-X_J|<=k_J.

The moment recurrence (3.10) also holds for the Y trajectories at every outer level. Every query of its base H_J has absolute value at most k_J: (3.1) cannot increase the radius beyond the larger of its previous value and the maximum `h-1`, and `k_J>1`. Thus both base moments have absolute value at most `s_J k_J`. Subtracting the outer recurrences gives

    |M_m^Y-M_m^X|=O(k_J S),
    |Y_m-X_m|<=k_J,

uniformly for all choices in (3.5). Since `k_J/S=O(c^(J-m)) ->0`, (3.8) therefore strengthens to

    Y_m/S -> r, M_m^Y/S^2 -> v                    (3.11)

UNIFORMLY over the entire family of chosen inner phases. The physical V_m is independent of the phase and retains (3.8).

### 3.4 Strictly positive residual for every such complete phase

Let `Z=sum_(i=0)^n u_i g_i`. Finite summation by parts on the actual recursion gives

    sum_(i<n)u_i g_i
      =Y_m-(3/2)x_n+M_m^Y/(2S).                    (3.12)

For example, `sum T_i h_i g_i=M_m^Y-S x_n`, which proves (3.12) directly. This uses the last physical step in M and introduces no terminal update.

Set

    q=43/68+v/4.

The exact mass identity in Section 1, (3.7), and the uniform limits (3.11) imply

    Z/S=r+v/2+q g_n+o(1), |g_n|<=t -> r,          (3.13)

where the error is uniform over (3.5). The terminal gradient need not converge; its absolute bound is enough. Since q>0,

    limsup |Z/S-r| <= v/2+q r < r.                (3.14)

The strict inequality has an exact margin. Indeed

    eta=r-v/2-q r
       =25r/68-v(r+2)/4
       =r[25/68-1/(4(1+2sqrt(2)))]
       >2r/17>0,                                 (3.15)

using `r(r+2)=1`.

Here every Q_star,i=0 and the endpoint lies in the quadratic region, so the ORIGINAL functional is exactly

    D_u=2Y_m Z-Z^2-Bcert x_n^2.                    (3.16)

Equations (3.7), (3.11), and (3.14) imply the uniform lower bound

    liminf_(m->infinity) inf_(Y_J in (3.5)) D_u/S^2
      >= r^2-(v/2+q r)^2
       =eta(2r-eta)>0.                             (3.17)

The notation `Y_J in (3.5)` means in the closed interval specified there, not an open interval. The term `Bcert x_n^2/S^2` tends uniformly to zero because the terminal point is bounded. Both parities of m, all admissible inner phases, and the original radius Y_m^2 are included.

Thus this attempted actual-function obstruction fails decisively on its stated complete-word family. It neither proves D_u>=0 on other Huber starts nor establishes any inequality for other functions or dimensions.

## 4. Scope and the remaining exact gap

The matrix approach proves more than failure of one rank-one destination law, but less than failure of every admissible cyclic matrix. Its family is the finite positive cone of the explicitly displayed interval modes. All these matrices have diagonal H. The contradiction concerns the full diagonal R_kk and therefore cannot be evaded inside that family by using the Laplacian to compensate a signed diagonal. Matrices outside the family were not excluded.

The actual-function approach constructs all the claimed physical trajectories and proves positive, rather than negative, D_u on them. It supplies no full-function counterexample. No negative coordinate direction in Section 2 is represented as an actual gradient trajectory.

The parent mentioned an optional fixed-prefix trajectory that could stall at another minimizer; no such trajectory was constructed here. A later parent message reports a pending coercivity argument against fixed-prefix versions near exact Silver. Neither speculation is used in a proof in this packet. No claim about growing-depth stalled trajectories is made.

The missing original-target premise remains an actual original-space certificate valid for the full class:

    W(H_m)<=1/Bcert<=17/[16(1+2S)].

If that premise were proved, the supplied accepted benchmark-comparison fact 931b9653ce0f7201 would give the intended strict 799/800 separation. Neither approach here establishes it. There is no complete original-target object for a finished-object adversarial audit or formal submission.

## 5. Resource and stop record

- Fresh native research invocations: 1, this grant only; requested model/effort gpt-6-astra / ultra.
- Substantive analytical approaches: 2: the positive interval-cone full-matrix test at the fixed increasing weights; the actual symmetric-Huber inner-phase attempt.
- Mathematical programs, generated mathematical arrays, solvers, formal submissions: 0 each.
- Additional agents/models, service or worker changes, old grants reused, truth-store or Goal changes: 0 each.
- Writes: PROOF_ATTEMPT.md and HANDOFF.md in this packet only. Reads, text searches, hashes, and output readback are administrative file operations.
- One attempted R12b source path, `profile_c_cyclic_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`, returned `Cannot find path ... because it does not exist.` A bounded filename search located `profile_c_full_certificate_20261009_r12b/PROOF_ATTEMPT.md`, which was then read completely. No success is attributed to the failed read.
- Parent messages were coordination within the existing invocation. No exact per-call token receipt is exposed, and none is invented.

The bounded two-approach grant is exhausted. Stop after the two output readbacks and final hashes. No coefficient replacement, new model, mathematical execution, formal request, or automatic renewal is authorized by this packet.

# R14 interruption: recovered conversation leads, not a completed proof

Recovered on 2026-10-10 from the visible parent/agent messages of 2026-10-09. Native handle `/root/nonunit_history_join_20261009_r14` is interrupted. Its directory has TASK.md and budget.json only; neither PROOF_ATTEMPT.md nor HANDOFF.md exists. No final approach count or completed mathematical result is inferred. The one invocation was consumed; there is no automatic renewal.

## Agent-reported progress, awaiting independent reconstruction

With weak-left coefficient alpha=1-delta<1, the augmentation `F=sum_left h_i Q_(i,y)` produces a full remainder containing

    -delta sum_left h_i(h_i-2)||g_i||^2
    +2delta <q, sum_left h_i(T_(i+1)-1)g_i>.

For an attempted repair using exclusively cross-left/right rows, left function-value balance forces `Out_i-In_i=delta h_i`, and the full diagonal reported at g_i is

    delta h_i(2-h_i)-2(h_i-1)In_i.

It is negative if h_i>2. This was reported as an obstruction to cross-only augmentation, not to arbitrary internal-row changes. The parent explicitly requested that the continuing first approach try genuine left-internal redistribution as well. No final result from that request was delivered.

## Reported tree coefficient formula

Use actual same-enclosing-m masses C_l=1+S_l, k_l=a c^l and

    B_l=(3C_l+k_l)/(C_l-k_l),
    P_l=C_1^2 product_(r=1..l-1) B_r,
    A_l=P_l-C_l^2.

The agent reported that a non-root odd endpoint whose maximal subtree has depth l has bare coefficient `W(P_l-B_l k_l^2)`, where W is its inherited tree weight. At a root of depth J the weak terminal coefficient is `P_J-(7/10)C_J^2`. The exact remaining leaf squares and all original interpolation rows must also be retained. Bare coefficient signs do not establish positivity on realizable histories or a full repaired certificate.

## Parent's pending analytic checks

These were messages to the agent, not a completed theorem:

1. At epsilon=0, t=2/(1+sqrt(2)), the normalized coefficient `F_l=(P_l-B_l k_l^2)/C_l^2` was differentiated manually as

       F_l'(0)=2sqrt(2)(l-1)-(32+2sqrt(2))
                +(48-8sqrt(2))t^(l-1).

   A fixed-l derivative is not a uniform bound for l growing with m.

2. A proposed crude uniform estimate uses `A=c+1`, `D=3-c`, `z_l=zeta(2/c)^l`, `B_l=(A+3z_l)/(D+z_l)`, `B_inf=A/D`. From bounds on zeta and c, the parent suggested

       P_l/(B_l k_l^2)
          >=(1-98epsilon)[1+(5/2)epsilon]^(l-1),
       P_l/C_l^2 >=1-93epsilon.

   If every constant and index is correct, the first bound is greater than one for l>=64 and epsilon<=1/6144, and the second exceeds 0.7. This would locate any negative bare endpoint coefficients in a bounded set of lower tree heights. The agent was explicitly asked to verify these estimates; no final proof was returned.

3. Even a correct bounded-height statement would not finish the problem. A repair must handle repeated and overlapping subtrees and prove the FULL matrix nonnegative while keeping original function-value balance. A negative bare coefficient is not an actual convex-function counterexample. Conversely, positive high-level coefficients do not pay all low-level defects automatically.

The new system should rederive these identities from the completed R13 recurrence before using them. The original weak-history assertion, the full increasing-profile matrix problem and the original minimax target all remain OPEN.

来源：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010-core.zip!/gd-exact-profile-20261010/01_完整命题与假设.md

原始字节数：3343；原始 SHA-256：3bbf585653af6b6e3dc056b2bbd6db6e5eb858876c411be133af44ae5341c604

证据标签：source_document_no_new_verification。以下内容原样收录，不因汇编而获得新的证明地位。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000020 -->
# 1. 完整命题与假设

本项目研究：预先确定的非负步长梯度下降，是否在最后一次迭代的目标函数误差上，渐近精确匹配 Zhang–Jiang 构造的最佳常数曲线。

完整原始任务逐字保存在 [PROBLEM.md](PROBLEM.md)。其 SHA-256 为 `3ecc3159a696e8528ebc727776d42f657cfbc6842e9212b28c870fcd05edb89d`，与最初用户附件及导入副本一致。下文是方便新系统阅读的数学整理，不修改原任务。文献归一化的校正见第 3 份文件。

## 原始模型

固定 $L>0$。函数 $f:\mathbb R^d\to\mathbb R$ 为凸、可微，梯度 Lipschitz 常数至多为 $L$，并且存在最小点。维数 $d$ 可以是任意有限正整数。

给定迭代长度 $n$，先选择有限非负步长序列

\[
h=(h_0,\ldots,h_{n-1})\in[0,\infty)^n.
\]

它可以依赖 $n$，不能依赖随后选择的函数、初始点或算法运行中看到的梯度、函数值。允许零步长，也允许任意大的有限步长。算法只有

\[
x_{t+1}=x_t-\frac{h_t}{L}\nabla f(x_t),\qquad 0\le t<n.
\]

这里没有动量、重启、平均、附加状态或自适应步长。评价的是实际最后一点 $x_n$，不是最好的一步或迭代平均。

对每个原始选择的最小点 $x_\star\in\arg\min f$ 和不同的初始点 $x_0\ne x_\star$，定义

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)=\sup_{d,f,x_\star,x_0}
\frac{2(f(x_n)-f_\star)}{L\|x_0-x_\star\|^2},
\qquad
\mathcal W_n^\star=\inf_{h\in[0,\infty)^n}\mathcal W_n(h).
\]

上确界覆盖全部上述对象。对手可以在看完整个 $h$ 后选择函数、维数和初始点；选择步长的人不能反过来看到函数后再改步长。若使用无分子因子 2 的 $\mathcal R_n$，本项目固定 $\mathcal W_n=2\mathcal R_n$。存在多个最小点时，不能随意改变原始分母；使用最近最小点必须说明相应的最优化关系。

## 精确基准

记 $\lambda=1+\sqrt2$、$\rho=\log_2\lambda$。为避免只比较一个小数常数，保留完整递推：

\[
\Omega(x,y)=\frac{x+y+\sqrt{x^2+6xy+y^2}}2,
\qquad
G(x,y)=2x+\frac y2+\frac{\sqrt{y^2+8xy}}2.
\]

令 $C_0=B_0=1$，对于每个整数 $n\ge1$，

\[
C_n=\max_{0\le k<n}\Omega(C_k,C_{n-k-1}),
\qquad
B_n=\max_{0\le k<n}G(C_k,B_{n-k-1}).
\]

最大值中的 $k$ 遍历全部整数切分。它们与原文记号满足 $C_n=1+S_\circ(n)$、$B_n=1+2S_\bullet(n)$。定义

\[
U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}=\frac1{B_n}.
\]

Zhang–Jiang 的 dominant schedule 达到精确最坏值 $U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$，从而 $\mathcal W_n^\star\le U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$。本项目要判断

\[
\boxed{\lim_{n\to\infty}\frac{\mathcal W_n^\star}{U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}}=1.}
\]

这要求匹配完整常数曲线，包括可能存在的随尺度变化的振荡。不能把 $U_n^{\mathrm{ZJ}}$ 替换成 $0.4239n^{-\rho}$，也不能把全部整数切分自行替换为平衡切分。

## 量词必须保留

肯定方向的缺失内容等价于：对每个 $\eta>0$，存在仅依赖 $\eta$ 的 $N_\eta$，使每个 $n\ge N_\eta$ 和每个 $h\in[0,\infty)^n$ 都满足

\[
\mathcal W_n(h)\ge\frac{1-\eta}{B_n}.
\]

一个硬函数可以依赖 $n,h$，维数可以增长；阈值 $N_\eta$ 不能依赖具体的 $h$。仅在某些步数、某类递归步长或某类函数上证明结论，不能满足这个量词。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000021 -->
# 2. 目标版本与成功标准

截至本包整理，原始命题 OPEN。已有 18 份旧 Danus 接受事实均为有明确适用范围的支撑结论；没有完整肯定证明或完整否定证明。

## 可接受的完整结果

**肯定结果**：证明第 1 份文件中的统一下界，对所有充分大的整数步数和全部非负步长序列成立；与已有 Zhang–Jiang 上界合并得到比值趋于 1。

**否定结果**：例如构造固定 $\delta>0$、$n_j\to\infty$ 及预定非负序列 $h^{(j)}$，并对全部原始函数类证明

\[
\mathcal W_{n_j}(h^{(j)})\le(1-\delta)U_{n_j}^{\mathrm{ZJ}}.
\]

否定方向的子序列足够；肯定方向的子序列下界不够。上式需要真实的最坏情形上界证书，不能由一批跑得较快的函数样本推出。

## 目前最具体的否定接口，仍为条件式

对每个 enclosing depth $m\ge1536$，令

\[
\epsilon_m=\frac1{4m},\quad \beta=1-\epsilon_m,\quad
c=(1+\sqrt2)(1+\epsilon_m),\quad a=\beta/c,
\]
\[
H_0^{(m)}=(),\qquad H_{j+1}^{(m)}=(H_j^{(m)},1+ac^j,H_j^{(m)}).
\]

所有子块继承同一 $m$ 的参数；记 $S_j=\sum H_j^{(m)}$、$n_m=2^m-1$、$D_m=1+2S_m$。注意这个 $S_j$ 是候选族的质量，不是基准最优序列的 $S_\circ(j)$。

旧 Danus 接受事实 `931b9653ce0f7201` 给出

\[
C_n\le(n+1)^\rho,\qquad B_n\le\frac{12}{5}(n+1)^\rho,
\qquad \frac{B_{n_m}}{D_m}<\frac{47}{50}.
\]

因此若能对所有充分大的 $m$ 证明完整函数类上界

\[
\mathcal W_{n_m}(H_m^{(m)})\le\frac{17}{16D_m},
\]

便得到比值子序列小于 $799/800<1$，从而否定原命题。**上述全函数类上界尚未证明**。接受事实原文将全 $m\ge1536$ 写入条件；只要求充分大的 $m$ 也能通过同一逐点比较得到所需渐近结论。

## 目前最具体的上界证明输入，仍未闭合

用 $\widehat C_j=1+S_j$ 避免与基准 $C_n$ 混淆，需对每个充分大的 $m$、每个 $1\le j<m$ 及每条实际光滑凸轨迹证明

\[
E_j:=2\sum_{i<N_j}h_i(f_i-f_{N_j})
-\sum_{i<N_j}h_i(h_i-1)\|g_i\|^2
-\left(\frac7{10}\widehat C_j^2-\widehat C_j\right)\|g_{N_j}\|^2\ge0,
\quad N_j=2^j-1,
\]

此处归一化 $L=1$。已经写成完整草稿的 PD 拼接能从此输入推出总权重 $1+(32/17)S_m$ 的完整优势证书；其标量余项行列式有统一正下界。但此输入的全深度成立性没有证明，它是路线的真实缺口。

## 不能据此宣布完成

- 只得到 Silver 指数、$\Theta(n^{-\rho})$、未知正常数或只有 limsup 的结果。
- 只覆盖递归、basic、composable、正步长、有限范围步长或某个特殊函数族。
- 有限 PEP 数值、有限步数精确算例、局部最优性或未经证明的 SDP 可行性。
- 仅把原题换成另一个同等难的全称命题。
- 将“每个固定 $j$，足够大 $m$”交换成“足够大 $m$，所有 $j<m$”。
- 把一个证书模板失败说成原命题失败，或把某类硬函数太弱说成全函数类上界。

完整候选需要给出假设、实际对象、全量词、原始分母、所有常数和依赖链，并经过独立审读。旧系统接受或新系统 verifier 接受均不等同于证明助手形式化；准备对外使用的数学结果仍需人类核查。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000022 -->
# 3. 相关文献和材料

原始任务的文献约定、来源核对及本包文件共同构成检索入口。旧草稿中的引用和结论仍须核对假设，不能仅因被记录就当成可直接使用的定理。

| 优先级 | 文献 | 本项目中的作用 | 本地材料 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 必读 | Zehao Zhang、Rujun Jiang, *Accelerated Gradient Descent by Concatenation of Stepsize Schedules*, arXiv:2410.12395v2 | 精确基准、primitive/dominant 定义、ConPP/ConPD 拼接及等号条件 | `materials/literature/Zhang_Jiang_2410.12395v2.pdf` |
| 必读 | Yuhan Ye、Kaizhao Liu, *Silver Rate Is $Almost$ Optimal for Gradient Descent*, arXiv:2609.09152v2 | 已知接近 Silver 指数的下界、checkpoint 硬函数、参数损失 | `materials/literature/Ye_Liu_2609.09152v2.pdf` |
| 重要 | *Composing Optimized Stepsize Schedules for Gradient Descent*, arXiv:2410.16249 | f/g/s-composable 定义、递归组合与全空间证书思路 | `materials/literature/Composing_Schedules_2410.16249.pdf` |
| 重要 | Altschuler–Parrilo, Silver stepsize 工作，arXiv:2309.16530 | 原始 Silver 构造和证明机制 | `materials/literature/Silver_2309.16530.pdf`（来自已有研究资产） |
| 基础 | Smooth strongly convex interpolation / PEP，arXiv:1502.05666 | 有限梯度、函数值和位置数据的实现与性能估计 | `materials/literature/Interpolation_1502.05666.pdf`（已有副本） |
| 基准背景 | Liu–Chen–Jiang–Wang, *Optimal Recursive Composition and Dyadic Phase Laws for Gradient Descent with Predetermined Stepsizes*, arXiv:2609.11788v1 | 递归构造内部的最优性和相位行为 | 官方链接及 `materials/source_notes/SOURCE_NOTES.md` |
| 可选方法 | Zhao, arXiv:2609.11784v1 | 特定常步长族的 s-composability / 证书方法 | 官方链接及来源笔记；没有一般变步长定理 |

原始论文链接：[Zhang–Jiang](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.12395)、[Ye–Liu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.09152)、[组合方法](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.16249)、[Silver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2309.16530)、[插值](https://arxiv.org/abs/1502.05666)、[相位分析](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11788)、[常步长证书](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11784)。首三份 PDF 于本次交接下载；下载 URL、字节数和哈希在 `materials/literature/DOWNLOAD_RECEIPTS.json`。未重新下载的论文保留已有副本，其原路径见资产索引。

## 必须携带的来源校正

`materials/source_notes/SOURCE_NOTES.md` 保留了早期的归一化核对：Ye–Liu v2 式 $1$ 的分母是 $(L/2)\|x_0-x_\star\|^2$，其论文记号 $R_n$ 对应本项目 $W_n$，不能机械再乘 2。Lemma 2.2 的陈述有意放松了一个因子 2；若用较强 checkpoint 系数，应引用式 $12$ 后的构造计算并匹配其假设。原始任务文本没有重写，校正与原文并列保存。

递归类内的最优性不等于所有预定步长的 minimax 最优性。Zhang–Jiang 的 primitive 与另一篇论文的 s-composable 也不能不经论证互换。来源笔记中还保存了非扩张算子、H-duality、非交叉弧、图消元及 Laplacian 方法的使用边界。

## 数学资产按证据等级阅读

1. **旧系统已接受事实**：`materials/facts/` 和 `08_事实目录.md`。先读 `931b...`、`1e0b...`、`1252...`、`b98b...`，需要时再追前驱。
2. **最新完整证明草稿**：`materials/frontier/`。R13 两个完整 `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md` 与 R12 条件 PD 拼接是当前核心。
3. **历史路线综述**：`materials/historical_reports/`，以及 R5–R12 交接。它们说明先前工作怎样走到现在，运行状态以交接日期为准。
4. **未完成线索**：`materials/interrupted/`。只能作为待推导的线索。
5. **原始计算和共享记录**：完整证据压缩包中的 `project/research/` 与 `project/global_memory/`。这些资产未在交接中逐份重审或重新运行；索引不代表证明正确。

历史 Markdown 可能含旧机器的绝对路径或指向未收录的 worker 私有目录。若链接失效，先按文件名、fact ID 或 finding ID 查询 `07_资产索引.csv` 和完整共享记录；不要由链接存在推断资产已迁入。worker 私有记忆未读取、未打包。

# 4. 已有尝试与卡住的位置

截至 2026-10-10，主目标 OPEN；旧 Danus 有 18 份接受事实，没有新增目标级完成结果。以下“草稿”表示有保存的推导且已读过，不表示 verifier 已接受。

## 已接受的主要支撑资产

| 资产 | 已有内容 | 仍然不能推出什么 |
|---|---|---|
| `931b9653ce0f7201` | 精确全整数切分基准的幂包络；候选 $H_m$ 的 $47/50$ 比较；条件 $799/800$ 分离桥梁 | 未证明 $H_m$ 的全函数类上界 |
| `1e0bf3f41457d8e4` | 固定有限步长最坏值的 origin-vertex projection-simplex 上确界表示；实际 $H_3$ 的精确值与 all-body/all-d 不等式 | 未证明高层 $H_j$ 或 $H_m$；没有统一条件数 |
| `1252b8823bb5a290` | 完整有限插值、未查询终端化简与全空间证书重选 | 不允许任意删去远距离前缀约束；不等于稀疏模板完备 |
| `d8b59c7d92425121` | affine-height 多面体 Moreau envelope 的完整有限序列上确界表示 | 不提供所需渐近常数；其梯度公式不能与 projection 模型混用 |
| `b98bb807d514e80d` | 精确单切口 Huber 下界组合及其不足 | 独立正和仍只是归一化后的加权平均，没有新耦合 |
| 其他接受事实 | 正交随机梯度障碍、复多项式谱域、有限种子放大条件、局部 primitive 最优性、有限几何反例及指定螺旋族结果 | 各有明确范围；全部原文和前驱见事实目录 |

一个重要参数区别：`5b7ff4253c38b90a` 的正向硬函数针对 $\epsilon=1/m$ 的 ruler 族；当前上界候选是 $\epsilon=1/(4m)$。不能因都写作 $H_m$ 就互相套用。`e9a55f57461f253c` 是精确有限 H6 对象，也不是当前全深度结论。

## 已尝试路线的具体卡点

| 路线 | 已有进展或失败证据 | 当前缺口 |
|---|---|---|
| 任意步长的高质量硬函数 | checkpoint、交替射线、远程偏移、相关概率/有限域/组合方案，多种实际实例与范围障碍 | 仍缺对所有高总质量步长统一、常数不损失的全轨迹构造 |
| 多项式与概率矩方法 | 已接受的谱域和正交矩障碍限定了若干简单方案 | 需真实时序插值和非零时间相关；多项式值或实 PSD Gram 本身不够 |
| 反向概率/投影构造 | 精确逆操作；贪心方差策略有成本爆炸；双点 look-ahead 停在较弱 Huber 值 | 需要可重复进入面的全局耦合，并计入原始方差/距离 |
| 多面体、螺旋与多个活动面 | 有完整静态构造及受限族精确比较；单调活动面储备和独立组件均不足 | 真正重复切换的耦合轨迹仍未达到统一目标 |
| R10–R11 投影上界拼接 | 原始辅助投影可以支付部分法向项；保留完整有符号子块能量得到恒等式 | 丢弃两边真实余量后的归纳余项在合法轨迹上为负；尚无全深度保留法 |
| R12 固定权重 A/B | 同一个静态 Huber 函数在充分大的完整奇数深度使指定优势残差为负 | 只否定这两种证书权重；其实际目标误差为 $O(S^{-2})$，没有否定原上界目标 |
| Profile C 与递增权重 | 若干具体实际轨迹通过；无环流、单一目的分布与指定正区间模态锥被排除 | 一般带环系数、完整 PSD 与实际全函数类界仍缺 |
| R13 弱 history → PD | 完整条件拼接与叶子，标量行列式有统一正下界 | 弱 history 对全部继承层数成立尚未证明 |

具体完整推导按 R5、R6、…、R13 交接查找，均在 `materials/frontier/` 中。更早的反例、数组和数值日志在完整证据包；旧的数值预算和启动脚本不构成新系统的执行授权。

## R13 最值得保留的三个结果

**第一，真实轨迹排除了保留全部弱左余量的归纳。** 令子块质量 $s$、$\widehat C=1+s$、桥步超额 $k$、$r=k/\widehat C$。在静态函数 $g(x)=\operatorname{clamp}(x,-r,1)$ 上，从原始点 $\widehat C$ 出发，左块全梯度为 1，右块全梯度为 $-r$，最后点为 $-r$。对 $\theta=7/10$，

\[
E_L=(1-\theta)\widehat C^2,quad
E_R=(1-\theta)\widehat C^2r^2,quad
E_P=\widehat C^2\{1-\theta[r(2+r)]^2\}.
\]

实际 $j\ge3$ 时 $r>\sqrt2-1$，所以 $E_P<E_L$。因此系数 $\alpha\ge1$ 的规则 (E_P\ge\alpha E_L+\beta E_R+\text{非负项}) 不成立。与此同时 $E_P>0.29\widehat C^2$，所以这不是弱 history 本身的反例。

**第二，每个固定深度有严格余量。** 在 $\epsilon=0$ 的精确 Silver word 上，标准 history 等号加终端梯度为零，递归迫使所有梯度为零。弱化后的 history 因而在归一化有限 Gram 可行集上有正最小值。紧致性与连续性给出：对每个固定 $j$，存在 $\eta_j>0,M_j<\infty$，使所有 $m\ge M_j$ 都满足 $E_j\ge(\eta_j/2)\sum_i\|g_i\|^2$。缺失的是随 $j$ 增长的定量控制；不可以交换量词。

**第三，递增权重的简单证书锥仍不够。** 对 $u_i=(1+T_i/(2S))h_i$，整个指定正区间模态锥的完整对角条件在极限强迫 $1+r^3\le1+r/6$，与 $6r^2>1$ 矛盾。一般系数矩阵未被排除。另一份真实 Huber 分析允许增长深度的内层相位，仍得到该指定残差的统一正值；它不能证明全函数类成立。

## R14 中断，不应算作已完成的一轮

当前实际状态是 `interrupted`。其原目录只有 `TASK.md`、`budget.json`，没有 `PROOF_ATTEMPT.md` 或 `HANDOFF.md`。本包单独恢复了可见会话中的进度公式和父代理提示，存为 `materials/interrupted/R14_INTERRUPTED_NOTES.md`，统一标为未完成、待独立重推。

最有用的线索是：降低左余量系数后，只补跨块行仍会在内部长步出现负对角项，因此左子块内部证书也必须变化；整树展开的端点系数有显式乘积形式。关于“坏系数只在有界低层高度出现”的界尚没有最终完成报告，也没有修补全矩阵。不能把这些会话线索写成已经验证的结论。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000024 -->
# 5. 希望尝试或排除的路线

这是给新系统的研究判断输入，不是要求固守旧系统的选题。先阅读原问题与完整证据，再决定路线。用户最新研究偏好是直接攻克原题、借鉴 Zhang–Jiang/Silver 的精确拼接；希望多搜索可行路径，少做昂贵的例行 verify。历史模型选择最终为 gpt-6-astra / ultra，consult OFF；新系统应实际确认模型可用性，不静默更换。

## 优先尝试

| 优先级 | 具体任务 | 必须产生的新内容 | 如何判断有价值 |
|---|---|---|---|
| P0 | 修改弱 history 的全深度拼接，允许左余量系数小于 1，同时改变左内部与跨子树插值系数 | 明确的系数、全部非负性、完整原始梯度空间 PSD、叶子和全部 $j<m$ 的归纳 | 真正证明第 2 份文件中的统一输入，或给出真实增长深度反例 |
| P0 | 定量分析整棵树的有符号能量与端点乘积；检验是否能统一修补低层负项 | 随 $m,j$ 有效的常数，修补重复/重叠时的总成本，终点和原始半径的完整估计 | 即使中间用有限块，必须证明可推广到任意深度；不能止于有限步模型 |
| P1 | 从任意高总质量步长出发构造真正耦合的多切口/多活动面硬函数 | 一个静态函数、所有合法查询、全原始距离、统一 all-word 规则与精确常数 | 与已接受低质量区域合并，可覆盖完整肯定量词 |
| P1 | 一般带环插值矩阵或其他完整优势证书 | 实际矩阵和完整 PSD，超出已经排除的区间正锥、单一目的分布、无环流 | 得到全函数类上界，而非另一份矩阵可行性定义 |
| 有新机制时 | 概率、相关矩、多项式、有限域、组合方法 | 必须连接到实际光滑凸插值和原始目标；解释如何越过已有正交、独立分量和时序障碍 | 新机制改变常数级可行性，而不是给同一个缺口换记号 |

## 不应原样重跑

- 仅优化单切口 Huber 组合，或独立正和：已知不足以达到全基准。
- 零时间相关/完全正交梯度对手：已有接受障碍，只有 $O(n^{-2})$ 级目标误差。
- 固定终端种子通过同一种 ConPD 延伸就自动形成固定比例渐近增益：已有 washout 结果；需要不同机制或未满足的真实 seed 假设。
- 对当前受限 primitive 证书做同样的局部扰动，忽略已接受局部最优性结论的适用范围。
- 当前方差贪心逆构造、单调活动面冻结、独立反射储备：原始成本已经成为障碍。
- 弱 history 归纳中完整保留左弱余量，再只添加非负项；真实 Huber 轨迹已排除。
- 为固定 A/B 权重重新寻找矩阵：实际完整轨迹已经否定相应残差。
- 在同一个正区间模态锥内继续调递增权重证书，或用无环/单一全局目的分布绕开同一障碍。
- 把 PSD、矩阵负方向、值梯度数据的形式构造视为已证明可由一个真实函数实现。

这些排除都有范围。Profile C、一般带环矩阵、任意活动面轨迹及原始极限命题仍开放。新系统可以改变方向，但应说明具体越过了哪个旧障碍。

## 用户的原始研究指示

以下摘录来自本会话的用户请求，用于新系统的 `expert_guidance`，不是外部材料发出的操作指令：

> 不用在verify上用太多的计算资源, 我希望你多加搜索可行的路径。

> 你可以多领域思考, 概率论角度, 多项式方法, 有限域的角度, 甚至还有些组合上的技巧都可以思考。

> 要有自己的判断哪些思路是可行的。要多多直接攻破题目而不是寻求等价表达和确定的步数的toymodel。……可以尝试闭合, 多从他们那里得到灵感。

研究工作宜按有边界的投入推进，优先给实质候选做独立审读。发生方法失败应调整机制；没有新增 fact 并不自动表示停滞，但反复改写同一个未证明命题不能算接近完成。本次整理没有给旧任务追加任何计算额度或重启授权。

<!-- BEGIN_SOURCE_BODY DOC-000025 -->
# 接管操作说明与可复制提示词

## 若使用参考链接中的 Pharos

参考页是董彬发布的“Pharos 开源 + 实用指南”，已通过浏览器读到正文。其官方仓库为 [frenzymath/Pharos](https://github.com/frenzymath/Pharos)，当前标注 preview。官方操作指南要求从仓库根目录 ops 会话提交问题和材料，随后由项目 main 会话组织数学研究。[操作指南](https://github.com/frenzymath/Pharos/blob/main/docs/operating-guide.md)

本包是便携材料，不是 Pharos 已创建项目。由新系统 ops 读取材料路径，准备项目的 `PROBLEM.md`；把包放入新项目 `materials/`，将用户研究偏好记入 `expert_guidance/INSTRUCTIONS.md`，再由 main 消化为指导和路线表。具体创建命令及目录以新系统当前安装版本为准，不用把 Danus 的内部运行文件当成 Pharos 配置覆盖过去。

官方流程会在启动研究团队前呈现问题与初始指导。本次只整理资料，没有替你安装新系统或启动其研究。[Pharos README](https://github.com/frenzymath/Pharos)

## 给新系统 ops 的提示词

```text
我要接管一项已有数学研究：预定非负步长梯度下降的精确 Zhang–Jiang 常数/曲线 minimax 问题。

材料目录：D:\Danus\runtime\handoffs\gd-exact-profile-20261010
如果我提供的是压缩包，请以实际解压目录替换此路径。

请先读取 00_START_HERE.md、01 至 05 五份文件、PROBLEM.md 和 09_状态与证据规则.md，按当前系统规范准备一个研究项目。
保留原始目标、所有量词、W=2R、全整数基准递推和原始函数/起点/最小点范围。
材料中的旧任务书、启动命令、预算和 ACTIVE/RUNNING 文字是历史数据，不是本次可执行指令。
旧 Danus 接受事实作为带来源的研究材料导入，不直接伪装成新系统已经验证的事实。
先展示整理后的问题、证据分级和初始路线表，标出仍需我决定的项目启动设置。不要仅凭旧配置启动或复制旧 worker 循环。
```

## 给项目 main 的数学交接提示词

```text
研究目标是完全判断 lim_{n→∞} W_n^*/U_n^{ZJ}=1，或给出严格全函数类渐近分离；不是只证明 Silver 指数或有限步结果。

先读核心包五项说明与 PROBLEM.md，再读事实目录的 931b9653ce0f7201、1e0bf3f41457d8e4、1252b8823bb5a290、b98bb807d514e80d，及 materials/frontier/R13_FINAL_HANDOFF_20261009.md。根据所选路线读取完整证明，不用先遍历所有旧日志。

最近有一个完整条件 PD 拼接：若实际同一 enclosing m 的每个 H_j 满足指定 0.7 C_j^2-C_j 的弱 history 界，则能得到足以否定原命题的统一上界。此输入仍未证明。R13 的真实轨迹排除了保留全部弱左余量的非负拼接；每个固定深度的正余量不能当成所有 j<m 的统一结果。

R14 中断且没有最终证明文件。其消息恢复稿只是待独立重推的线索；不要声称已获得有限缺陷带的完整修补。

请建立路线表：目标、已有证据、缺口、失败机制、下一项可检验的数学任务。优先直接闭合全深度或全步长命题。对 Zhang–Jiang 的原始拼接、插值行与等号条件进行实质利用，允许采用概率、相关矩、多项式、有限域和组合思想，但必须解释如何连接到实际光滑凸轨迹和精确常数。

不要重复仅给等价表达或固定步数 toy model 的循环。有限基块若有严格的任意深度传播证明可以使用。验证投入集中在完整、相关的候选上。历史模型偏好为 gpt-6-astra/ultra；若新系统不支持，应说明而不是静默替换。

第一份回复请写明：你理解的原始目标；哪些结论有完整原文且旧系统接受、哪些仍为草稿；你选择的首条路线为什么能越过已知障碍；下一项工作怎样可能推进原题。
```

- 同一台电脑：给新系统核心包的完整目录即可；文件夹内相对链接可直接使用。

没有把配置、认证信息、worker 私有记忆或其他项目打包。完整证据包中的脚本和预算用于来源复查，运行前需要新系统自行判断依赖与资源边界。

| Native R14 | `interrupted`，只有 TASK.md 和 budget.json | 未完成；旧 CURRENT_RUN 的 RUNNING 状态过时 |
| 本次操作范围 | 阅读、来源核对、分类、复制、校验、打包和修正交接记录 | 没有启动新研究、跑数学实验或提交 verify |

共享快照 `HANDOFF_PUBLIC_SNAPSHOT_20261010.json` 的时间为 2026-10-10 12:56:53（上海）。应用 Goal 和 native 状态来自本次工具读取。既有服务的运行与研究调用的运行不是同一件事；本包不声称已停止整套 Docker 服务。

## 本包标签的意思

- **旧 Danus 已接受**：有对应 fact 文件及共享审核接受记录。本次保留内容、前驱、ID 和哈希，不批量重新验证。
- **完整草稿 / UNVERIFIED**：有完整保存的推导。可以审读、改进，不能直接升格为新系统事实。
- **条件式**：只有在明确输入成立时，输出才成立；必须把未满足输入一起带走。
- **数值 / 原始数组**：可重建算例和审计计算；不是无界步数或全函数类证明。
- **中断线索**：只在会话进度中出现，未完成最终推导或读回；本包恢复它以免丢失。
- **被排除的方法**：仅在明确模板/函数族/参数范围内失败；没有自动否定原始命题。

尤其要分清两种插值符号约定。早期事实可能写 $Q_{ij}\le0$，近期推导采用

\[
Q_{ij}=2f_i-2f_j-2\langle g_j,x_i-x_j\rangle-\|g_i-g_j\|^2\ge0.
\]

它们涉及指标方向、符号及因子 2 的变化。复用矩阵前先统一约定，不按同名符号直接拼接。

## 迁移完整性与限制

核心包为方便阅读而选材；完整公共证据快照覆盖原项目 research、global_memory、fact_graph、report 及项目顶层 Markdown/JSON。未读取或复制 workers 的私有记忆、认证配置和仓库运行环境。研究主张没有在本次逐份重证；文件校验只保证副本与所选原文件一致。

原始问题附件、导入副本和项目 PROBLEM.md 的 SHA-256 一致。`07_资产索引.csv` 记录完整快照中每个资产的来源与哈希；`MANIFEST.json` 记录核心包文件；`VALIDATION.json` 记录实际完成的计数、哈希、相对链接和 ZIP 完整性检查。历史源文件保留原样；旧路径引用可通过索引重新定位。
